- `d1a1`: Queen c3 attacks a1. (c3-b2-a1) - No.
- `d1d3`: Captures Bishop, but lost Rook.

Queen at c3. Rook at b1. Not on the same row (1 vs 3), not on the same column (b vs c). Diagonal: c3-b2-a1. b1 is not on that diagonal.
